ideas = {
	branch = {
		law = yes
		#FRANCE
		#FRANCE - TOTAL ENERGIES
		FRA_totalenergies_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea FRA_totalenergies_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = FRA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					FRA = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				FRA = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.075 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					USA = {
						set_temp_variable = { modify_economic_crisis_oil = 1 }
						modify_economic_crisis_oil_support = yes 
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = FRA
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = FRA
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = FRA
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = FRA
					has_war_with = FRA
					is_in_array = { FRA.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = FRA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = FRA modifier = medium_decrease }
				FRA = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.075 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_france_total_idea
			
			modifier = {
				country_resource_oil = 2
				production_speed_rubber_refinery_factor = 0.03
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_france_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					FRA = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = FRA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = FRA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = FRA
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -300
					has_war_with = FRA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = FRA
						has_war_with = FRA
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#SAUDI BRANCHES
		#SAUDI - ARAMCO
		SAU_aramco_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea SAU_aramco_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SAU }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAU modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAU modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					SAU = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				SAU = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.075 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					USA = {
						set_temp_variable = { modify_economic_crisis_oil = 1 }
						modify_economic_crisis_oil_support = yes 
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				hidden_trigger = {
				IRQ = { NOT = { has_country_flag = IRQ_arabco_created } }
				}
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SAU
				}
			}
			allowed = {
				IRQ = { NOT = { has_country_flag = IRQ_arabco_created } }
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SAU
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = SAU
				IRQ = { NOT = { has_country_flag = IRQ_arabco_created } }
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					IRQ = { has_country_flag = IRQ_arabco_created }
					is_embargoed_by = SAU
					has_war_with = SAU
					is_in_array = { SAU.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SAU }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAU modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SAU modifier = medium_decrease }
				SAU = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.075 }
						modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_saudi_aramco_idea
			
			modifier = {
				country_resource_oil = 3
				production_speed_rubber_refinery_factor = 0.03
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_saudi_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					SAU = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = SAU
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = SAU
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = SAU
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -300
					has_war_with = SAU
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = SAU
						has_war_with = SAU
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		#RUSSIAN BRANCHES
		#RUSSIA - Gazprom
		SOV_gazprom_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea SOV_gazprom_branch_idea"

					}
				}
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.075 }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {

						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_idea = ARM_gaz_energy_problems
					}
					remove_ideas = ARM_gaz_energy_problems
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					USA = {

				}
			}

					is_embargoed_by = SOV
					has_war_with = SOV
					is_in_array = { SOV.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.075 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_sov_gazprom_idea
			
			modifier = {
				country_resource_oil = 2
				production_speed_rubber_refinery_factor = 0.03
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_russia_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					SOV = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = SOV
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = SOV
						has_war_with = SOV
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }

		#RUSSIA - Rosneft
		SOV_rosneft_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea SOV_rosneft_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					SOV = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.075 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					USA = {
						set_temp_variable = { modify_economic_crisis_oil = 1 }
						modify_economic_crisis_oil_support = yes 
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = SOV
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = bedniy_eblan
					NOT = {
						has_idea = Non_State_Actor
					}
					SOV = {
						NOT = {
							has_completed_focus = SOV_economic_reform_rosneft_nationalize
						}
					}
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = SOV
					has_war_with = SOV
					is_in_array = { SOV.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.075 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_sov_rosneft_idea
			
			modifier = {
				country_resource_oil = 2
				production_speed_industrial_complex_factor = 0.02
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_russia_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					SOV = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = SOV
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = SOV
						has_war_with = SOV
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					SOV = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.055 }
						modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = SOV
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_idea = Non_State_Actor
					}
					has_country_flag = SOV_loyal_puppet
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = SOV
					has_war_with = SOV
					is_in_array = { SOV.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.055 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_sov_evraz_idea
			
			modifier = {
				country_resource_steel = 2
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_russia_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					SOV = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = SOV
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = SOV
						has_war_with = SOV
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}

								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SOV
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = SOV
				SOV = {
					has_completed_focus = SOV_found_rosatom
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_idea = Non_State_Actor
					}
					has_country_flag = SOV_loyal_puppet
				}
				if = {
					limit = { original_tag = POL }
					has_completed_focus = POL_rosatom
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = SOV
					has_war_with = SOV
					is_in_array = { SOV.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = medium_decrease }
				SOV = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_russia_rosatom_idea
			
			modifier = {
				nuclear_energy_generation_modifier = 0.01
				nuclear_fuel_consumption_modifier = -0.01
				repair_speed_nuclear_reactor_factor = 0.05
				production_speed_nuclear_reactor_factor = 0.05
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_russia_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					SOV = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = SOV
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = SOV
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = SOV
						has_war_with = SOV
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ENG }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ENG modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = ENG modifier = medium_decrease }
				ENG = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_armenia_hsbc_idea
			
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				economic_cycles_cost_factor = -0.03
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_english_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					ENG = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = ENG
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = ENG
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = ENG
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = ENG
						has_war_with = ENG
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}

					ENG = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = ENG
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = ENG
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = ENG
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = ENG
						has_war_with = ENG
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#ENGLAND - Unilever
		ENG_Unilever_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea ENG_Unilever_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ENG }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ENG modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = ENG modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					ENG = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				ENG = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = ENG
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = ENG
					has_war_with = ENG
					is_in_array = { ENG.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ENG }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ENG modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = ENG modifier = medium_decrease }
				ENG = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_england_unilever_idea
			
			modifier = {
				consumer_goods_factor = -0.03
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_english_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					ENG = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = ENG
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = ENG
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = ENG
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = ENG
						has_war_with = ENG
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					USA = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				USA = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = USA
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = USA
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = USA
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = USA
					has_war_with = USA
					is_in_array = { USA.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				add_to_variable = { global.ethernet_count = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = medium_decrease }
				USA = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_usa_nvidia_idea
			
			modifier = {
				research_speed_factor = 0.04
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_america_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					USA = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = USA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = USA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = USA
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = USA
						has_war_with = USA
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = USA
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = USA
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = USA
					has_war_with = USA
					is_in_array = { USA.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				add_to_variable = { global.infrastructure_count = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = USA modifier = medium_decrease }
				USA = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_usa_citygroup_idea
			
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				economic_cycles_cost_factor = -0.02
				receiving_investment_cost_modifier = -0.01
				investment_duration_modifier = -0.01
				return_on_investment_modifier = 0.01
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_america_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					USA = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = USA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = USA
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = USA
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = USA
						has_war_with = USA
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}

								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_china_lenovo_idea

				research_speed_factor = 0.02
				receiving_investment_cost_modifier = -0.01
				investment_duration_modifier = -0.01
				return_on_investment_modifier = 0.01
				production_speed_offices_factor = 0.01 
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_china_branches_tt
			}

		CHI_Agricultural_Bank_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea CHI_Agricultural_Bank_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				add_to_variable = { grain_count = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = CHI }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = CHI modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = CHI modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					CHI = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				CHI = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = CHI
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = CHI
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = CHI
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = CHI
					has_war_with = CHI
					is_in_array = { CHI.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				add_to_variable = { grain_count = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = CHI }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = CHI modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = CHI modifier = medium_decrease }
				CHI = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.045 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_china_agriculturebank_idea
			
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				economic_cycles_cost_factor = -0.04
				agricolture_productivity_modifier = 0.05
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_china_branches_tt
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {

						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {

				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = CHI
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = CHI
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = CHI
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = CHI
						has_war_with = CHI
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#ARMENIA BRANCHES
		#ARMENIA - Inecobank
		ARM_economy_inecobank_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea ARM_economy_inecobank_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ARM }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ARM modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = ARM modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				ARM = {
					country_event = redux_generic.15
				}
				ARM = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				original_tag = NKR
			}
			allowed = { 
				original_tag = NKR
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = ARM
					has_war_with = ARM
					is_in_array = { ARM.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = ARM
				has_country_flag = ARM_approves_branches
				ARM = {
					has_country_flag = ARM_economy_inecobank_idea1_yes
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ARM }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ARM modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = ARM modifier = medium_decrease }
				ARM = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_armenia_ineco_idea
			
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				economic_cycles_cost_factor = -0.02
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					ARM = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = ARM
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = ARM
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = ARM
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -300
					has_war_with = ARM
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = ARM
						has_war_with = ARM
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				original_tag = NKR
			}
			allowed = { 
				original_tag = NKR
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = ARM
					has_war_with = ARM
					is_in_array = { ARM.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = ARM
				has_country_flag = ARM_approves_branches
				ARM = {
					has_country_flag = ARM_economy_marianna_idea1_yes
				}
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				add_to_variable = { grain_count = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ARM }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ARM modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = ARM modifier = medium_decrease }
				ARM = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_armenia_marianna_idea
			
			modifier = {
				agriculture_workers_modifier = 0.02
				agricolture_productivity_modifier = 0.02
				consumer_goods_factor = -0.01
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					ARM = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = ARM
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = ARM
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = ARM
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -300
					has_war_with = ARM
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = ARM
						has_war_with = ARM
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}

							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					POL = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = POL
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = POL
						has_war_with = POL
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}

		POL_PGE_branch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea POL_PGE_branch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POL }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					POL = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				POL = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			visible = {
				original_tag = LIT
				has_country_flag = LIT_pge_branch
			}
			available = {
				original_tag = LIT
			}
			allowed = { 
				original_tag = LIT
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = POL
					has_war_with = POL
					is_in_array = { POL.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_poland_pge_idea
			modifier = {
				energy_use_multiplier = -0.01
				energy_gain_multiplier = 0.01
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_poland_branches_tt
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POL }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = medium_decrease }
				POL = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {

					}
				}
				modifier = {

					is_in_faction_with = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POL
				}
				modifier = {

						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {

		#BELARUS - MZKT
		BLR_mzktbranch_idea = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea BLR_mzktbranch_idea"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = BLR }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = BLR modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = BLR modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					BLR = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				BLR = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.002 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = BLR
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = BLR
				}
			}
			visible = {
				country_exists = BLR
				NOT = {
					has_idea = Non_State_Actor
				}
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = BLR_branches_activate
					is_subject_of = BLR
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = BLR
					has_war_with = BLR
					is_in_array = { BLR.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_belarus_mzkt_idea
			equipment_bonus = {
				heavy_tank_chassis = {
						build_cost_ic = -0.02
				}
				medium_tank_artillery_chassis = {
					build_cost_ic = -0.02
				}
				util_vehicle_equipment = {
					defense = 0.02
					reliability = 0.01
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_belarus_branches_tt
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = BLR }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = BLR modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = BLR modifier = medium_decrease }
				BLR = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.002 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					BLR = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = BLR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = BLR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = BLR
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = BLR
						has_war_with = BLR
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SLO modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SLO modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					SLO = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				SLO = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			visible = {
				NOT = {
					tag = SLO
				}
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SLO
				}
				SLO = {
					has_completed_focus = SLO_sygic
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SLO
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = SLO
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = SLO
					has_war_with = SLO
					is_in_array = { SLO.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = SLO_sygic_company
			modifier = {
				production_speed_internet_station_factor = 0.12
				production_speed_offices_factor = 0.02
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_slovak_branches_tt
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				add_to_variable = { global.ethernet_count = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SLO }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = SLO modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SLO modifier = medium_decrease }
				SLO = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					SLO = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = SLO
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = SLO
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = SLO
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = SLO
						has_war_with = SLO
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			visible = {
				NOT = {
					tag = POL
				}
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POL
				}
				POL = {
					has_completed_focus = POL_alior_bank
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POL
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POL
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = POL
					has_war_with = POL
					is_in_array = { POL.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_poland_aliorbank_idea
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				economic_cycles_cost_factor = -0.03
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_poland_branches_tt
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POL }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = medium_decrease }
				POL = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					POL = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = POL
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = POL
						has_war_with = POL
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

				add_to_variable = { global.ethernet_count = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POL }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = medium_decrease }
				POL = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					POL = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POL
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = POL
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = POL
						has_war_with = POL
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		POL_t_mobile_branch = {

				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POL modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					POL = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				POL = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			visible = {
				NOT = {
					tag = POL
				}
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POL
				}
				POL = {
					has_completed_focus = POL_rebranding_era
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POL
				}
			}
			allowed = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POL
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = POL
					has_war_with = POL
					is_in_array = { POL.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = generic_company_poland_tmobile_idea
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				production_speed_internet_station_factor = 0.12
				production_speed_offices_factor = 0.02
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_poland_branches_tt
			}

						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					POR = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = POR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = POR
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = POR
						has_war_with = POR
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }

		POR_bpf_branch = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea POR_bpf_branch"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POR }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					POR = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				POR = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			visible = {
				NOT = {
					tag = POR
				}

					original_tag = POR
				}

				}

					original_tag = POR
				}

				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = POR
					has_war_with = POR
					is_in_array = { POR.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = POR_bpf_company_idea
			modifier = {
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				inflation_defence_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				economic_cycles_cost_factor = -0.02
				foreign_influence_modifier = 0.01
				foreign_influence_defense_modifier = 0.01
				receiving_investment_cost_modifier = -0.06
				investment_duration_modifier = -0.06
				return_on_investment_modifier = 0.02
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_portugal_branches_tt
			}
			on_remove = {

				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -1 }

				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = medium_decrease }

							index = temp_index_to_remove

								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					POR = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = POR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					has_opinion = {
						target = POR
						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = POR
						has_war_with = POR
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
					check_variable = { count_branches = 6 }
					any_country = {
						is_in_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						is_top3_influencer = no
						PREV = {
							has_opinion = {
								target = PREV
								value < 80
							}
						}
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = PREV }
						NOT = { PREV = { is_subject_of = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
