		#Portugal - Almina
		POR_almina_branch = {
			on_add = {
				log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: add idea POR_almina_branch"
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 1 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POR }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = large_commercial_relations }
				if = {
					limit = { 
						OR = {
							has_idea = great_power
							has_idea = large_power
							has_idea = regional_power
						}
					}
					POR = {
						country_event = redux_generic.15
					}
				}
				POR = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								is_in_array = {
									array = countries_with_branches
									value = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = PREV
						}
						add_to_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							value = 1
						}
					}
					else = {
						for_each_loop = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							value = v
							index = i
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
								add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = 1 }
								clamp_variable = { var = countries_with_count_branches^i min = 1 max = 6 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			visible = {
				NOT = {
					tag = POR
				}
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POR
				}
				POR = {
					has_completed_focus = POR_almina
				}
			}
			available = {
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POR
				}
			}
			allowed = { 
				NOT = {
					original_tag = POR
				}
			}
			cancel = {
				OR = {
					is_embargoed_by = POR
					has_war_with = POR
					is_in_array = { POR.sanctions_targets = ROOT }
				}
			}
			picture = POR_almina_company_idea
			modifier = {
				production_lack_of_resource_penalty_factor = -0.02

				tungsten_export_multiplier_modifier = 0.04
				corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.01
				custom_modifier_tooltip = GENERIC_portugal_branches_tt
			}
			on_remove = {
				add_to_variable = { count_branches = -1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = count_branches min = 0 max = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = POR }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
				change_influence_percentage = yes
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = medium_decrease }
				reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = POR modifier = medium_decrease }
				POR = {
					set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  -0.005 }
					modify_capitalization_support = yes
					for_each_loop = {
						array = countries_with_branches
						value = v
						index = i
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { v = PREV } }
							add_to_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^i = -1 }
							set_variable = { temp_index_to_remove = i }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove = 0 }
								check_variable = { countries_with_count_branches^temp_index_to_remove < 0 }
							}
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
						remove_from_array = {
							array = countries_with_count_branches
							index = temp_index_to_remove
						}
					}
					clear_variable = temp_index_to_remove
				}
			}
			ai_will_do = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					POR = { 
						is_top3_influencer = yes 
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_in_faction_with = POR
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					is_subject_of = POR

						value > 80
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -500
					OR = {
						is_embargoed_by = POR
						has_war_with = POR

				}
				modifier = {
					add = 500
